### SW-CG2-20261019-C-001 — Competence and public legibility

**Initial formulation:** Monica's extreme technical competence and difficulty under human observation coexist as distinct, condition-dependent processes; technical success does not confer general social, bodily or institutional mastery, and her reserve-admission belief is not a reliable measure of her magical standing.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE, supported by OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT and NARRATOR_PRESENTATION; the reserve account is CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF. Scope: examinations recalled two years earlier through V01 exit. Support: E003 (roughly 80% spell coverage, examination workaround and unverified pity inference); E005 (rank knowledge); E008–011 (failed introductions, human-shaped Nero, abrupt numerical fluency); E018 (flight); E022 (reporting, exhaustion); E023 (Louis's combat defeat, orderly scholarly writing). Strongest rival: her supposed incompetence is only exaggerated comic staging around a hidden all-purpose genius. Counterevidence to that rival is repeated practical failure and narrated domain limits. Counterevidence to an overstrong deficit reading is voluntary thanks, successful detective reasoning, recipient-specific ease and fluent technical argument. Uncertainty: selectors' exact rank order is unrevealed; no diagnosis, fixed disability threshold or global cognitive profile is warranted. Revise if later admitted evidence distinguishes a changed disposition, a new role or only newly revealed competence. Master S-MONICA, W001–002, K003.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-002 — Numbers as plural activity

**Initial formulation:** Numerical attention is both intrinsically/paternally pleasurable and an available retreat from threat; it also connects Monica to the world and people, so neither escapism nor dispassionate rationality alone explains it.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE with direct NARRATOR_PRESENTATION of thoughts/memories. Support: E005 pig counting; E009 assault-associated dream; E010 father's tape, coffee/ribbon/nuts and waste question; E011 golden-ratio proof; E012 forensic calculation; E015 fan strike deepens retreat; E021 pig dream reverses refuge into imposed fear. Strongest rival: a single obsession explains all these cases without meaningful motivational variation. That compression loses the narrated affective contrast between discovery, avoidance, pride and care. Counterevidence to benevolent universalization: attention excludes interlocutors, exposes her cover and can facilitate disproportionate retaliation. Uncertainty: exact relation between a remembered past and all present habits remains incompletely disclosed. A later episode where numerical absorption has another mechanism should expand this claim rather than be forced into one function. Master T002/T004/T006.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 — Ordinary reciprocity and bounded agency

**Initial formulation:** Monica's early movement toward affiliation is substantially carried by ordinary reciprocity—recognizing attention, thanking, requesting instruction and attempting an imperfect skill—rather than by a simple increase in fame or power.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE. Support: E008–010 failed then achieved thanks, ribbon and nuts becoming valuable, Lana's mirrored fear of boring speech; E012 concern and navigation; E016 Lana's worry after appointment and acknowledgment of provocation; E018 keeping the hairstyle; E022 continued learning and welcomed role address. Strongest rival: coercive exposure and office alone produce the change, with friendship only decorative. Counterevidence: Monica chooses the request to Lana and cherishes its result privately before office; rank/publicity intensify fear. Counterevidence to an uncomplicated friendship cure: Lana remains sharp/intrusive, recognition feeds her vanity too, Monica continues failing and fearing loss. Uncertainty: durable transfer to other recipients and autonomous confidence beyond V01 are untested. Future refusal, reciprocal repair, ordinary disagreement or unobserved chosen contact would discriminate. Master R004, S-LANA, Q001/Q009.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-004 — Cyril's divided response

**Initial formulation:** Cyril's loyalty to Felix, demand for earned recognition and procedural sense of duty produce different responses: punitive intimidation toward perceived disrespect can coexist with fair inquiry, careful teaching and acknowledgment of Monica's work.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE; private recognition history is NARRATOR_PRESENTATION under his focalization. Support: E006–007 anger at Aaron; E012 injury check and Monica's protective false account; E016 independent audit; E017 meticulous instruction, recognition exhortation and witness-based censure of Caroline; E019 adoptive selection, family history and overtraining; E022 inhibited rebuke and escort as treasurer. Strongest rival: his severity is merely hostility/class prejudice that the ending abruptly cancels. Counterevidence: witness checking predates final acceptance, competence recognition precedes social approval, and his modest background complicates automatic old-rank allegiance. Rival in the other direction: duty fully excuses cruelty. Threats and inability to understand fear contradict that. Uncertainty: his suspicions of rescue identity are not confirmed and cannot be treated as the established sole cause of the escort. Subsequent treatment under error or explicit identity disclosure could change the causal weighting. Master S-CYRIL, R005/R007.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-005 — Felix's care and control

**Initial formulation:** Felix's attention to Monica combines practical help and interest in anomalous competence with deliberate surveillance, unwanted handling and a private project of taming her hidden purpose; V01 does not establish reciprocal romantic intention or simple benevolence.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE; his game/wish are CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF plus private NARRATOR_PRESENTATION. Support: E009 rescue/nuts; E011 identification and inquiry; E014 mercy qualified by concealment; E015 keys, Will, kiss, cookies, appointment and “game”; E017 badge/chin/ownership language; E022 hair repair and role pressure; E023 ignorance of her sage identity alongside reverence for Lady Everett. Strongest rival: conventional princely flirtation expresses straightforward care. It accounts for some aid and romantic comparison but not his explicit recognition that she is frightened, game language or concealed testing. Counterevidence to pure cynicism: he appreciates surprise, protects Will, shoulders Cyril and has an unexplained view of his limited remaining life. Uncertainty: depth/nature of admiration, intended endpoint and role constraint remain unresolved; future identity disclosure or conduct when control fails would discriminate. Do not infer romance from bystanders' interpretation of bodily proximity. Master S-FELIX/R006/T007, Q003–004.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-006 — Louis's mixed commission

**Initial formulation:** Louis selects Monica for genuine professional fit and exceptional power, while coercion, concern for his own standing, enjoyment of challenge and some practical care all shape his conduct; his later confidence-building explanation does not make the commission consensual or wholly altruistic.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE; royal-intent and low-execution-risk assessments remain CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF. Support: E002–005 divergent treatment of Annie/Monica/Rosalie, protective-tracking device, refusal denied, arranged disguise; E018 written report channel and family announcement; E022 sweets recalled as support; E023 defeated pride, backup disciple, private admiration, dragon-outing motive and monitoring theory. Strongest rival: he is either a pure bully or secretly a wholly benevolent mentor. Each misses substantial counterevidence: professional planning/recognition versus knowingly terrifying leverage and amusement. Uncertainty: king's actual intent, backup identity and how much Louis's explanation is self-serving are unresolved. Later independent royal evidence or care without reputational/professional payoff could discriminate. Master S-LOUIS/R001/W008, Q005/Q008.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-007 — Proof, confession and institutional truth

**Initial formulation:** V01 repeatedly separates what can be proved, what a vulnerable person confesses, what authority presents publicly and what privately happened; neither rank nor a total confession guarantees truth, while procedural inquiry and mundane records can correct misclassification.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE with OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT and attributed reports kept distinct. Support: E006 Aaron's real fraud and missing collaborator; E012/017 Caroline's rank claim versus Cyril's witness work; E014 Selma's identity needs and expansive self-blame, contrasted with pot/glove/application evidence; E021–023 Thornley interference and official illness/arrest accounts. Strongest rival: the academy mystery reaches a transparent confession-based solution. Its apparent solution is materially reopened by evidence of memory interference and the report's account of manipulation. Counterevidence to “institutional truth is always false”: record checking, room applications, witnesses and published arrest carry real corrections. Uncertainty: Selma's confession to the sign attack has less independent material support than the pot case; no fully independent victim recovery or final sentence is shown. New testimony must be attributed rather than silently used to certify earlier knowledge. Master K004–009/W006–007/T003.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-008 — Focalization and plural silence

**Initial formulation:** The novel's changing focalizers and delayed private disclosures make silence, gentleness and apparent foolishness unstable signs; explanations are revised through access to motives and knowledge rather than a universal public/private key.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE about form. Support: E001 public miracle versus E002–003 hut life; E006 Aaron's memory hole; E008–011 intended speech versus public failure; E014 Selma; E015 Bridget/Monica/Nero/Felix view shifts and private game, real recreation versus bait cover; E016 Cyril's interrupted continuity; E019–020 monster perspective; E023 brooch, selection and final identity irony. Strongest rival: a single omniscient comic narrator simply labels everyone while withholding arbitrary surprises. Explicit shifts alter the ethical and causal interpretation of already-seen behavior, and corrections are recipient-specific. Counterevidence: narration sometimes gives direct explanatory generalizations and obvious comic corrections, so not every statement is unreliable or restricted. Uncertainty: later formal pattern cannot be asserted from V01 alone. A later disclosure must not rewrite the PRE_V01 zero state or earlier scene knowledge. Master T001/T003/T005/K002–010.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-009 — Perfection and the ethics of force

**Initial formulation:** Precision can preserve others but is not itself proof of ethical or legal adequacy; Monica's climactic use of a perfected mental formula remains qualified by her anger, the subject's terror and her immediate fear that she overdid it.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE plus bounded NORMATIVE_EVALUATION. Criterion: consent, proportionality, effects on affected persons and viable alternatives must be considered independently of technical beauty or comic success. Support: E001 controlled dragon descent; E012 partial optimization of a fall; E015 failed violent awakening; E019 protected attacker/forest and repaired aid; E021 perfect butterflies, twenty-four-hour pig dream, uncertainty about statutory exception and Lin's concealment maxim; E023 lingering pig utterances/possible disposition. Strongest rival: harmless comic punishment of an attacker, fully authorized by sage status. Actual assault, threatened mental destruction and finite duration support a defensive reading, but no external ruling settles legality and perceived suffering is explicit. Counterevidence to a claim of indiscriminate cruelty: she protects Cyril while he attacks and selects limited confinement rather than the destructive formula. Uncertainty: lasting effects and exact alternatives considered are not shown; do not invent them. Master W003–005/T006, Q007.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-010 — Recognition as resource and peril

**Initial formulation:** Recognition is a contested condition of worth in V01: it can enable shared action, become dependence on acceptance, or turn threatened prestige into coercion. Monica's wish that Cyril not call his talent a curse does not establish that she can cease doing so herself.

Class: STRONG_INFERENCE drawing distinct processes together, not treating subjects as identical. Support: E003 Monica's self-estimate; E010 mutual topic shame and teaching; E014 Selma's “nothing” and fiancé attachment; E017 lost friend/pride refusal; E019 Cyril's selection, mother/aid and Monica's repair motive; E021 Thornley's envy and escalation; E022 earned treasurer address; E023 Louis's injured standing. Strongest rival: talents simply become good once others applaud them. Cyril's dangerous overtraining and Selma's willingness to surrender everything undermine this; Monica's past friendship makes applause itself threatening. Counterevidence to an exclusively harmful model: paternal listening, Lana's instruction and Cyril's final escort offer enabling acknowledgment. Uncertainty: the father's loss is not causally explained and should not be made into a general theory of her history. Later failure, refusal, unsolicited recognition and ordinary repair can test whether acceptance remains conditional. Master T004/R004–005, Q001/Q006/Q009.

### C001 — current via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-001

**Current formulation:** Monica's technical competence, public fear and bodily/social limitations remain condition-dependent processes; practice and particular relationships now enable selected embodied, interpersonal and political actions, without establishing general mastery or validating her reserve-admission belief.

Repeated bodily danger, lack of food, wrong conversational timing, help from several specialists and exhausted collapse defeat that generalization. Rival in the other direction: every gain is compelled compliance. Chosen requests, private practice and interruption of Louis resist it. His later evidence-sensitive protection is real but retains asymmetrical debt language, so rescue and concealment do not establish reconciliation or an unchanged wholly hostile motive.

Class STRONG_INFERENCE with remembered experience, private thought, action and speech separated. Scope SW-LNJP-V03, including its retrospective Minerva scenes. Support V03:E001–003/E012/E014/E016–019; p-002:P4–269, p-007:P199–393, p-008:P58–246. Strongest rival: justifiable merit grievance gives way to full repentance once she saves him. His wish for enduring fear, engineered encounter, absence of apology and final debt demand resist that sequence. Opposite rival: protection is merely worthless manipulation. He identifies a genuine danger, risks himself and supplies a useful cover she lacks. Uncertainty: the mixture of pride, residual affection, obligation and rescue response is not fully disclosed at the lie; future conduct may distinguish them. Routes S-BARNEY/R016, T009, L021, Q014. This is a new V03 claim, not a fabricated V01–V02 history.

### C002 — current via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-020

**Current formulation:** Monica's numerical attention has distinct aesthetic, paternal, archival, bodily and defensive uses; it can exclude persons and bodily need, but a shared sequence can also reconnect her to a person. V04 distinguishes deliberately translating a hostile familiar face into quantities from her inability and ethical refusal to reduce valued people that way, and from her local choice to retain an imperfect experience rather than displace it with numbers.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** from focalized NARRATOR_PRESENTATION, represented memory, OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT and quoted self-directed judgment. Registered scope **V01–V04**, retaining earlier support through RV010. Added support **V04:E001–003/E027–031/E037/E041**: childhood danger and Hilda's numerical bridge (p-002:P4–112); active impersonal reframing against Yuan; hostage attention collapse; Casey's receipt and rejected excuse (p-014:P183–201); selected dance memory (p-016:P165–177); maxim's different hostile uptake (p-019:P99–115). The last is formal comparison, not proof of another person's same cognition.

Strongest rival: a final conversion from inhuman calculation to feeling makes prior numbers false/evil. Counterevidence: numerical beauty remains explicitly highest and technical analysis still protects; Hilda reaches her through it. Opposite rival: social attachment is superficial, and the earlier extreme retreat is the sole authentic self. Chosen memory and inability to devalue particular lives refute exclusivity. Uncertainty: no diagnosis, stable threshold, guarantee against future retreat or complete paternal-record/trial knowledge. “Cannot” is bounded to her present valued people and hostage situation, not an abolished cognitive capacity. Reconsider if a later freely chosen act deliberately depersonalizes a loved person, or a changed setting gives numbers a distinct role. Routes master S-MONICA/R023, T012/T014, L029/L034/L037.

### C003 — current via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-021

**Current formulation:** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through ordinary reciprocity, practical accommodation, selected responsibilities and refusal of some demanded recognition. Particular attachments now supply diagnostic knowledge and chosen purpose while also exposing her to hostage leverage, concealment guilt and a useful-self demand that can prevent asking for help; voluntary enjoyment is a real gain without completed recovery or independence.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; scope **V01–V04**. Earlier registered support through RV011 retained; added **V04:E008/E012/E014/E016/E024/E027–031/E034/E036–038**. The original self/other asymmetry is visible in accepting partial help for another house's secret, inviting Ray without force, defending Cyril through experienced acts, detecting the counterfeit address/brooch/ribbon, losing vigilance over the true Cyril, and declining a transport helper after poison. Her final memory choice is made within anticipated loss and cover, not imposed by Louis's guard duty.

Strongest rival: friendships simply cure inhibition and make good choices automatic. Counterevidence: she conceals illness, overexerts and collapses; care can make the hostage threat work. Opposite rival: attachment is therefore a mistake or every choice only coercion. Particular familiarity exposes the intruder, Ray returns by his own choice and rescues her, and she chooses private dance/ball memories. No completed identity disclosure, repaired old false blame, independence or reciprocal romance. Reconsider on an actual help request, honored refusal or consequence of revealing cover. Routes S-MONICA/R001–006/R012/R016/R024, W030/T012, L032/L034/L037, Q018.

AFTER04 newly discloses particular childhood injuries behind his general rule that crossing rank brings unhappiness, an additional history he withholds partly to protect a friend, and his continuing application of the tragedy rule; causal intelligibility and friendship do not amount to egalitarian conversion.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** grounded in CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF, separately focalized memory/present thought and OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT; the rule is his belief, not a verified social law. Scope **V02–V04 + AFTER04**, with V01 antecedent still at its historical route. All RV025 support retained; add **AFTER04:E014–015/E018**, p-012:P318–490/P730–775 and p-013:P1–45. Narrated childhood abuse and suicide memory differ from Benjamin's suggestion of a second intensification, which remains unspoken. A seven-year-old friend's accommodation is actual past care; no unprovided identity solution follows.

Strongest rival: explanatory injury justifies rank prejudice or proves Elliott was always secretly egalitarian. His repeated generalization and Bridget remark counter the latter; one betrayal does not establish the rule's universal truth. Opposite rival: continuing prejudice cancels all V04 change and friendship is empty hypocrisy. The prior private admission and present protection of Benjamin remain affirmative evidence. These are disclosures at different points, not a straight restoration of an old absolute state. Reconsider on disclosed second history, treatment of a nonexceptional lower-status person in conflict, explicit revision of the rule or a costly decision applying it. Routes S-ELLIOTT/R008/R032/L048–049/Q009.

### C012 — same formulation, strengthened via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-030

**Current formulation:** Hospitality, help and gifts depend on recipient, resources, occasion and unequal knowledge of convention. Material benefit, affection, comprehension, informed uptake and public obligation can diverge: a wanted memory need not remove giving pressure, the same rose can mean courage or political betrothal, and a wanted public affiliation with another differs from imposing ownership on them.

Exact RV026 wording retained. Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** with the same bounded **NORMATIVE_EVALUATION** based on actual benefit/refusal, speaker/recipient position, permission and imposed handling. Scope of registered support now **V02–V04 + AFTER04**. Earlier support remains; add **AFTER04:E004/E007/E017/E022/E024–026**, especially p-004:P1–44, p-006:P621–686, p-012:P643–727, p-015:P642–741, p-018:P63–157 and p-020:P1–38. Sheila's own account independently confirms the blue-thread meaning previously only hypothesized by Monica. Byron explicitly values the gift without reading that meaning. His card's successful material reconstruction gives Sheila an invitation while Monica retains the charm account. Actual Hilda receipt differs from Monica's intended sending; Lana's thread choice makes burden a giver's considered concern.

Strongest rival remains that sincere, expensive or gratefully accepted giving guarantees shared meaning and assent. New direct disclosure supplies a discriminating contrary instance rather than merely another attractive object. Opposite rival remains that etiquette/resources never matter or all unshared comprehension invalidates benefit. Kept hairpin, preserved rose, good portions, handmade card and received thanks counter it. No objectively operating courage charm, actual accepted dance, universal gendered gift rule, or exoneration of uninvited touch follows. Routes R004–006/R028/R036–038/W035/T016–018/L043/L045/L051–052/Q022.

### SW-CG2-20261019-C-019 — Protective containment and manufactured inevitability

**Initial/current formulation:** Isabelle's quiet resolution protects immediate school life and the implicated girls from a feared royal-curse escalation by recovering the book and curating disclosure. Immediate reduced danger, return/restriction of the dangerous book, Monica's stated aim and genuine grateful relief counter that. ## V05 current formulations after material review

### C002 — numerical purposes and the risk of chosen truth

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-002, current via RV032.** Monica's numerical attention has distinct aesthetic, paternal, archival, bodily and defensive uses; it can exclude persons and bodily need, while a shared sequence can reconnect her to a person. Face-reduction is now explicitly situated after the uncle's violence, without making all numerical pleasure its symptom. V04's ethical refusal to reduce valued people and choice of imperfect memory remain; V05 adds deliberate measurement in pursuit of a friend's and mother dragon's truth, then a filial research purpose. A chosen purpose can redirect numerical skill without proving its conclusions impartial or displacing retreat.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, with OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT/NARRATOR_PRESENTATION and separately attributed intentions/memory. Scope: original V01–V04 support retained plus V05 E013 (excluded conference words), E024–025 (failed refuge, measured accusation), E030 (explicit face chronology and new research possibility). Registered boundary now V01–V04 plus V05; AFTER04 remains a local comparison rather than an invented intermediate registration. Strongest rival: a single defensive obsession either wholly cured by friendship or wholly unchanged. Counterevidence: function-specific successes/failures and changed stated purpose. Material uncertainty: father's maxim does not certify father's innocence or the impartiality of filial investigation; no diagnosis or general face-recognition profile. Affected master S-MONICA/T019/L059/L063/Q025.

### C003 — affiliation enables action and competing projects

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-003, current via RV033.** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through ordinary reciprocity, practical accommodation, selected responsibilities, bounded help and refusal of some demanded recognition. Attachments supply knowledge and chosen purpose while exposing her to leverage, concealment guilt and useful-self pressure. V05 turns professional exchange, graduated teaching and assembled expertise into planned action, then an explicitly chosen filial investigation with hired help. That project can conflict with guarding/maintaining the admired sage ideal and can itself reproduce withholding; effective choice does not establish completed recovery, independence or equal understanding.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, supported by actions, focalized wants/appraisals and narrator constraints. Prior V01–V04 + AFTER04 support retained; add V05 E003/E010–012/E016–017/E020/E024–026/E029–031. Strongest rival: sociability and stronger magic amount to uncomplicated autonomous growth; opposite rival every offered relation is merely coercion. Counterevidence: wanted future research, specific teaching and shared work coexist with panic, forced exposure and selectively informed collaboration. Uncertainty: permanence of new employment and investigation, honest response to contrary evidence, next-year commitment and complete cover costs remain open. Affected S-MONICA/R006/R019/R024/R038–039/T019/L054–055/L059/L063/Q018/Q025.

### C004 — senior care, learned steadiness and received courage

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-004, current via RV034.** Cyril's earned-worth demands, loyalty and procedural duty coexist with recipient-specific care that creates time and room for imperfection, without cancelling intimidation, mistaken assumptions or withheld information. Maternal misrecognition helps explain the pursuit of praise. He deliberately witnesses and trusts particular juniors despite risk; V05 lets him receive a mother's recognition of the steady work learned from her and, under his own fear, draw courage from his juniors' earlier resolve. Senior responsibility can therefore circulate recognition rather than only demand or supply it; neither received reassurance nor rank advancement proves a completed repair.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; actual dialogue/action plus private appraisal and independently disclosed maternal history. Prior RV022 V01–V04 set retained. Newly registered support **now**: recovered AFTER04 E009–012, specifically E010/p-009:P676–709 for doubt → defense → chosen senior trust, and V05 E003/E027/E031 for safe witness, received mother answer and returned junior example. This admission does not pretend that AFTER04's repair advanced C004's support at that earlier date. Strongest rival: severe loyalty is the whole process, or a kindly ending cancels intimidation. Counterevidence: particular protection/learning and continuing anticipatory dread need different contexts. Uncertainty: old root cause still not wholly explained to him; no Everett identification/universal egalitarianism. Master R005/R022/R030–031/T021–022/L053/L060/L064/Q019.

### C005 — imperfect scholarship, protection and prepared control

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-005, current via RV035.** Felix's care and interest combine intellectual admiration, remembered vulnerability and recipient-specific responsiveness with possessive pressure, surveillance and unequal knowledge/risk. V05 discloses a serious scholarly aim that welcomes Everett's exact criticism and an explicit memorial project for Ark; actual rescue concern can alter an immediate death choice without displacing that greater aim. Protective acts and revised reverence coexist with prepared misleading answers, bodily-response tests and plans to trace the witch. ### V06 material formulations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 — current via RV038.** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through ordinary reciprocity, accommodation, selected responsibilities, bounded help and refusal of demanded recognition. Attachments support planned action and an independently chosen investigation while exposing her to leverage, useful-self pressure, concealment guilt and competing duties. V06 makes ordinary continuity itself a chosen good threatened by rank and discovery: friends' familiar conduct without demanded confession lets her ask to remain, while newly recognized anger for them motivates intervention despite continuing fear. Being a sage does not extinguish a child's claim to ask for help outside employment. None of these gains establishes equal understanding, impartial inquiry, general confidence or recovery.

Bartholomeus's fee refusal is real and still has a matchmaking condition. Reconsider after an actual identity disclosure, an unprofitable help request or contrary filial evidence. ### V06 new registration

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-022 — Glen: praise, prolonged pain and practical learning. Initial formulation, registered only at V06.** Glen's wish to become useful and heroic does not make his cheerful presentation transparent evidence of ease. V06 newly dramatizes how class contempt and praise-seeking exposed an eleven-year-old with very large reserves but weak foundations to Hubard's abusive “safe” practice: greater reserves prolonged pain rather than supplying mastery. The current Glen conceals pain to spare others, yet receives and practices specific foundational help, adapts care to recipients and cooperates under real danger. Neither his earlier runaway nor present learning proves innate violence, a painless cure or a single cause for every cheerful act.

### V07 current material formulations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-006 — current via RV042.** Louis's commission combines professional fit, self-interest, coercion and practical care. V04's failed heartlessness model prompted real fault acceptance and accommodation of attachment. V06–V07 rescue and technical cooperation coexist with institutional autonomy and suppression of a conscious witness. V07 makes attachment itself a testable mission cost: Louis pressures Monica to seal her friends' memories, presupposing safe technical competence rather than obtaining their consent; the narrator confirms the test. Mary prevents performance and her declared case closure bars later intervention and sanctions. Louis respects that limit while retaining selective disclosure and political self-interest. Neither competent rescue nor stopped coercion establishes equal partnership, all-knowing benevolence, a general legal acquittal or a performed memory erasure.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** with proposed speech/testing intent/actual cessation separately **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT / NARRATOR_PRESENTATION**. Preserve RV041's registered **V01–V04 + specified V06** and separately retained convent qualification; add **V07 E006/E010–012/E014**, decisive p-015:P38–78/P109–113. No automatic AFTER04/V05 admission. Strongest rival: harmless readiness rehearsal controlled in advance by benevolent seniors. Explicit pressure, Monica's objection and Mary's separate interruption resist that; the opposite total-cruelty rival cannot erase the real rescue and accepted limit. Unknown: what Louis would have done without Mary, full legality, old victim outcomes and political end state. Routes S-LOUIS/R001/R049–050/K062/W053/T032/L082/Q007/Q010/Q033.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-005 — current via RV043.** Felix's scholarly admiration, care and remembered vulnerability coexist with desired dependence, surveillance, secret lethal means and unequal risk. V06's killing order remains actual and unfulfilled; his approval of Will's changed judgment is real. V07 adds a protection offer to Darwin whose force depends on feared Clockford punishment, and a failed interpretation of Bridget's rumor as Everett's strategy. He can read one vulnerable person's alternatives accurately while misidentifying another agent. Bridget's remembered weak prince's kindness and the gardener's scarred servant's gifts/punishment deepen the history at stake; Monica's combined name, language, bodily-manipulation and household clues now support a strong substitution hypothesis. Neither that hypothesis nor the childhood suffering constitutes a complete identity/operation account or excuses present elective coercion. No explicit Felix join of Norton/Everett, mutual romance, total control or harmless protective necessity is established.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, maintaining distinctions between actual current conduct, **NARRATOR_PRESENTED MEMORY**, gardener's **CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF**, Monica's focalized conclusion and analyst convergence. Preserve RV040's **V01–V04 + specified V05/V06** support; add **V07 E015–017/E021–022**, principally p-015:P247–300; p-016:P150–324; p-017:P84–132; p-018:P4–230; p-020:P181–407; p-021:P33–128. The scarf tests simple ignorance; the translated names, reported death, learned clues and distinct independent retrospective complicate the illness-only account. No definitive performed substitution or reliable corpse identification appears. Uncertainty: precise original prince/servant fate, surgeon/commission/orders, the present speaker's own account, Ark/Ike relation and remaining-life condition. **SW-CG2-20261019-C-015 — current via RV044.** Mary's expert observation and divination enable real protection within limits, while her guidance also imposes tests, managed information and unequal alternatives. V07 makes her authority a practical check on Louis: she stops the proposed memory sealing and declares further intervention and sanctions prohibited. Her substitute closure nevertheless commands silence, withholds the flute account and asks the rescued boys to entrust forest management to her name. Her coordination and materially equipped workshop for Bartholomeus have useful effects and conditions; her wish not to let an unnamed person die supplies a private stake without proving a fully controlled plan, omniscience or harmless benevolence. The beneficiary's identity and the loss-bearing omen remain unresolved.

### V07 first registrations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-023 — respect for a value not yet shared. Initial registration only at V07.** Monica's attempt to understand Bridget's dangerous devotion moves from a universal model toward attention to different recipients. Chess, musical beauty, bodily measurement and philosophy fail to supply a common rule; Claudia's desired specialness and Lana's calm companionship do not become Monica's own identical desire. She can instead take Bridget's motive seriously and offer bounded practical cooperation without first explaining or adopting it. Numerical exactness still produces a valid spatial finding; its limit is determining why another person matters. This is a changed mode of appraisal, not cured anxiety, universal empathy, accepted romantic identity or proof that every intrusive inquiry is justified.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, supported by narrated self-questioning and **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**, not a psychological diagnosis. Scope **SW-LNJP-V07 only**, including Monica's recalled appraisal of Selma. Origin/support **E017–021**, chiefly p-018:P4–230; p-019:P4–189; p-020:P4–180/P370–373; p-021:P33–79. Strongest rival: she merely acquires a standard romantic script; unlike wants and continuing uncertainty counter it. Opposite rival: lack of a definition means no change; her explicit refusal to handle Bridget's uncomprehended motive carelessly and actual collaboration counter that. Counterevidence: chosen truth seeking can violate Ike's wish for privacy, and both helpers retain withheld aims. Reconsider on an actual conflicting request/refusal, abandoned inquiry or a recipient rejecting her interpretation. Routes S-MONICA/S-BRIDGET/R004/R027/R043/K064–066/T033/L084–087/Q031/Q038.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-024 — particular reciprocity without species absolution. Initial registration only at V07.** The forest's partial rescue depends on acts directed to particular beings without a common account of innocence, debt or species. Cyril cannot absolve Romaria's killing but fulfills the frozen-flower promise as a gift; Monica preserves him for Cyril's sake without claiming impartial justice or a consensual contract. Sezdio prevents renewed violence while refusing thanks. These changes coexist with human exploitation, coerced abduction, Reruva's irreversible death and the released spirits' collective hostility. Effective care can interrupt violence without establishing mutual understanding, universal trust or equivalence between all human-spirit relations.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, grounding in **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**, narrator-presented past and attributed recipient language, not a universal moral law. Scope **SW-LNJP-V07 only**; V06's promised flower/coercion remain antecedents at their existing routes. Origin/support **E003–005/E008–009/E012/E014**, especially p-007:P4–159; p-014:P169–316; p-015:P79–108. Strongest rival: all humans are interchangeable exploiters and apparent rescue is merely a different ownership. Refused compulsory offering, explicit self-interested friendship motive, a fulfilled gift and Sezdio's chosen restraint resist it. Opposite rival: a kind human cancels every grievance and saves everyone; Reruva's death, Romaria's culpable past, unconsented emergency preservation and remaining hostility resist it. Uncertainty: Romaria's later will/voice, Sezdio's enduring position and Mary's management. Reconsider on actual consent, refusal, renewed violence or an explanation of another controlled spirit's fate. ### V08 current material formulations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 - current via RV045.** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through ordinary reciprocity, accommodation, selected responsibility, bounded aid and refusal, while attachment exposes her to leverage, useful-self pressure and concealment guilt. Ordinary continuity is itself a desired good. V08 tests protecting it by unilateral secrecy: personal understanding and actual material limits lead her to ask adults for specific help, then knowingly risk Norton life by naming Everett and asking the assembled school group for strength. The disclosure is her chosen means under urgent constraints, not evidence that fear is cured or acceptance guaranteed. Some collaborators still receive incomplete terms; their answers, the rescue and the future of ordinary life remain unperformed.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; request/disclosure **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**, wants/costs **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION / focalized appraisal**. Preserve RV038's **V01–V04 + AFTER04 + specified V05/V06**; add **V08 E002/E004/E024–026/E028–032**, decisive p-016:P25-82/P247-257 and p-017:P157-187/p-018:P4. V07 remains local comparison, not silently registered now. Strongest rival: forced crisis merely extracts compliance from an unchanged solitary self. Explicitly chosen purpose, rejected recruitment, bounded requests and deliberate identity risk resist it. Opposite friendship-cure rival loses exhaustion, guilt and unreceived response. Uncertainty: acceptance, full informed consent, actual war/rescue and duration. Reconsider on Q042's received refusal/cooperation. Routes S-MONICA/R001–006/R012/R019/R038/T041/L094/L098/L101/Q011/Q018/Q031/Q042.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-004 - current via RV046.** Cyril's earned-worth demands, loyalty and procedural duty coexist with addressed care, intimidation, mistakes and selective information. Received recognition can return courage rather than only reward his juniors. V08's ten-year fraud exposes the title beneath his chosen loyalty as false; he nearly strikes, then collapses into denial of his own worth. Monica's protest preserves the value of his actual conduct and reminds him that choosing and being chosen exceeded family rank. He continues entrusted council work without settled forgiveness. His spirit study responds to experienced ignorance, while his harsh claim that earlier neighborliness was not kindness remains a self-appraisal qualified by actual help. Neither reassurance, study nor eventual title recognition proves complete repair or expertise.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, distinct actual actions/reported selection/private judgment. Preserve RV039's **V01–V04 + specified AFTER04/V05/V06**; add **V08 E018/E020–023/E030**, chiefly p-013:P341-415 and p-016:P119-158. V07's gift remains local, not an implied new registered set. Strongest rival: his loyalty simply transfers intact because a junior consoles him. Actual collapse, articulated inconsistency and continuing unsettled feeling counter that. Opposite rival all work is delusion loses genuine ability/selection and choice. Severity toward Glen, cat-call misrecognition and self-blame limit a progress narrative. Unknown: response to known Everett, personal reconciliation, maternal full explanation and practical learning. Routes S-CYRIL/R005/R007/R030–031/R045/T038/L094/Q002/Q019/Q039/Q042.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-005 - current via RV047.** The present Felix is Isaac Walker, whose real care and intellectual delight coexist with surveillance, desired dependence, deception and elective lethal means. Direct V08 history places the duke's replacement project before the original prince's death, then shows Isaac electing a memorial role within coercion, grief and self-blame. He makes Ark's ideal-prince list a program overriding the friend's stated freedom wish, and reserves personal happiness for a finite pre-accession life. This history explains constraints without excusing his intimidation or later assassination delegation. Learning whose father died newly gives Norton-specific guilt and an inability to wish her eliminated, counterevidence to his own all-sacrifice rule. He nevertheless plans to die a fabricated imperial villain so his achievements remain Ark's. His delight that Everett can remember Isaac's name shows a personal wish within self-erasure. Norton/Everett remain unjoined for him; neither mutual romance, blanket innocence nor total control is established.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** for mechanism; identity/death/operation and present ignorance explicitly **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION**, diary and reports classed separately. Preserve RV043's **V01–V04 + specified V05/V06/V07**; add **V08 E008–017/E019/E021/E027**, decisive p-007–010, p-012:P105-288, p-015:P151-219. Do not silently admit AFTER04 to registered support. Strongest rival: coercion/trauma make all later manipulation involuntary or harmless. Elected continuation, assault, deliberated assassination and selective exceptions contradict it. Opposite sham-care rival loses actual friendship, pleasure, protection and guilt. Earlier V07 hypothesis was warranted but incomplete then, not falsely narrated certainty. Unknown: future diary uptake, confession, changed methods or identity-joined conduct. Routes S-FELIX/S-ISAAC-PAST/S-ARK/R006–007/R011/R017/R055/R061–064/K071–074/T037–038/L092/Q003–004/Q017/Q034/Q041/Q043.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-006 - current via RV048.** Louis combines professional fit, self-interest, practical care and coercion, with real recognition of attachment rather than an unchanged heartlessness model. V07's proposed memory-sealing test remains stopped and barred. V08 shows that understanding feeling can instead justify withholding a political plan from the attached person. He correctly doubts Darwin's suicide while accepting a convenient false account to avoid suspicion against Lionel's faction. Familial care and helplessness with his daughter coexist with strategic severity toward Monica. Her decision to hide the diary and work around him marks a substantive divergence over the expendable accused, though his imagined response to that unoffered evidence remains her forecast. Neither rescue, political insight nor fatherhood establishes equal consultation or benevolent control.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** supported by narrated strategy, actual exchange and recipient-specific appraisal. Preserve RV042's **V01–V04 + specified V06/V07**; add **V08 E017/E020/E026**, p-011:P150-192, p-013:P4-41, p-015:P4-118. AFTER04/V05 stay earlier local qualifications, not automatic registered scope. Strongest rival: perceptive mentor must share Monica's true end and only stages an educational obstacle. Explicit faction interest, withheld plan and her deliberate secrecy resist it. Opposite pure-cruelty rival loses practical care, actual earlier model correction and family conduct. Unknown: his response to project/diary, king's motive and acceptable compromises. Routes S-LOUIS/R001/R010/R028/R049–050/K074/W060/T040/L097/Q005/Q031/Q045.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-011 - current via RV049.** Elliott's inherited rank hierarchy licenses exclusion and protective self-risk, while actual exceptional peers and his own conduct create conflict within it. Earlier abuse/uncle history and the bounded V04 qualification remain. V08 discloses the second childhood history: kindness from the bullied real prince earned admiration; the detected replacement then used violence, family threats and displaced blame while confessing the prince's death. Elliott's watching for ruin therefore includes loyalty, violated trust and class disgust, not one motive alone. Present Cyril/Monica counterexamples undermine total contempt without abolishing the hierarchy or proving all boundary-crossing tragic.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, narrator-presented memory/character doctrine differentiated. Preserve **V02–V04 + AFTER04**; add **V08 E010–012/E021–023**, especially p-013:P146-340. Strongest rival: real injury validates a universal birth rule or reveals secret egalitarianism. Neither follows; continuing generalization and actual lower-status competence oppose both. Opposite no-change rival loses explicit conflict. The new history is disclosed now, not retroactively assigned to earlier inference. Uncertainty: conduct toward a nonexceptional person, costly public choice and future hatred. Routes S-ELLIOTT/R008/R017/R032/T038/L093/Q009/Q041.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-012 - current via RV050.** Hospitality, aid and gifts depend on recipient, resources, occasion and unequal knowledge; material benefit, affection, comprehension, uptake and obligation diverge. V08 adds delayed use: retained objects can enable a new act through remembered care, recovered evidence or changed interpretation without guaranteeing the giver's intention reaches its addressee. Casey's handkerchief helps renewed thanks, Cyril's dried rose activates reliance, the watch reveals Isaac's contractual name, and preserved papers enable a still-unbuilt device. Mercy's preserved diary also withholds its message for years. Worth and survival of an object do not themselves establish delivery, consent, shared meaning or effective manufacture.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE** with bounded **NORMATIVE_EVALUATION** retaining benefit/refusal/permission/recipient criteria. Preserve RV030's **V02–V04 + AFTER04**; add **V08 E001/E024–025/E028–031**. V05–V07 comparisons remain their original local routes. Strongest rival: heartfelt preservation guarantees faithful fulfillment; missing delivery, divergent wish interpretation and fabrication limits counter it. Opposite mere-symbol rival loses performed handkerchief/rose/watch/design consequences. Unknown: diary receipt, actual Grail function and recipient response. Routes R005/R012/R020/R038/R063/K073/K076/T039/L090/L096/L098/L100/Q022/Q043–044.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-013 - current via RV051.** Casey's deliberate assassination remains grounded in family obligation and concretely received protection under unequal regional security; a renewed friendship does not retract its manipulation, danger or unsettled necessity. V08 develops the residual relation into actual dialogue. Both women hesitate over whether the other still cares, the retained embroidered handkerchief permits thanks and familiar laughter, and Casey gives personal permission for Monica's chosen course while pursuing her own practical convent life. Relation can be rebuilt after a real break without making the earlier betrayal a false mask or the present exchange a complete political reconciliation.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; actual exchange/food work **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**, inferred privacy/feelings remain attributed. Preserve **V02–V03 through RV019**; add only **V08 E024–025**, p-014:P101-176/P344-402. Strongest rival: the initial friendship was wholly tactical or, conversely, renewed warmth vindicates assassination. The retained gift and mutual caution oppose the first; unwithdrawn crime/history oppose the second. Casey's “permission” is personal novice speech, not legal pardon. Unknown: future conflict, network, exhaustive agreement compliance and acceptance of Monica's means. Routes S-CASEY/R012/R014/R067/L096/L100/Q010/Q031.

### First registration after V08

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-025 - preserving a wish can appropriate its recipient. Initial registration only.** In V08, devotion to a dead person's remembered wish can become a program that defeats that person's expressed concern for another's individual life. Isaac's ideal-prince list, the prince's freedom request, deliberately avoided happiness and the missing last diary page show selective preservation with more than one cause: coercion, grief, guilt, unavailable words and chosen interpretation. Mercy's fearful preservation delays the addressee; Will's transitive wish-serving and proposed self-loss offer related but nonidentical limits. Monica's refusal of another sacrifice and turn toward addressed requests open an alternative without accomplishing freedom by information alone.

Scope **SW-LNJP-V08 only**, including newly narrated past at its actual disclosure date. Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, not diagnosis/universal law; acts and narrator appraisals separately grounded. Origin **E010–015/E024/E027/E031**, especially p-009:P144-264, p-010:P4-278, p-014:P239-340 and p-016:P211-257. Prior list/memorial clues remain antecedents in their witnesses, not fabricated original support. Strongest rival: one hidden message alone causes the tragedy and its discovery will cure it. Isaac deliberately avoided a freedom wish he already knew; coercive institutions and ongoing elective violence give independent causes. Opposite rival all devotion is manipulation loses actual friendship, practical care and willingness to help. Will is not simply equated with Clockford or Isaac. The final corrective sentence is not printed; its exact content/uptake cannot be claimed. Reconsider on delivered reading, refused command, changed self-wish or another receiver's dissent. Routes S-FELIX/S-ARK/S-MERCY/S-WILL/R061–064/K071–073/T037/L092/L096/L099/Q043/Q046. No prior claim wording or initial revision event exists.

V06 makes ordinary continuity itself a chosen good threatened by rank and discovery: friends' familiar conduct without demanded confession lets her ask to remain, while newly recognized anger for them motivates intervention despite continuing fear. Being a sage does not extinguish a child's claim to ask for help outside employment. None of these gains establishes equal understanding, impartial inquiry, general confidence or recovery." **SW-CG2-20261019-RV-046 - C004, REVISE.** Prior exact **RV039** wording: "Cyril's earned-worth demands, loyalty and procedural duty coexist with recipient-specific care that creates time and room for imperfection, without cancelling intimidation, error or withheld information. Maternal misrecognition helps explain his praise-seeking; received recognition and juniors' example can return courage to him. V06 adds an explicit practical distinction between legitimate need and nonconsensual means: he hears the spirits without pardoning abduction, consults Glen before committing their help and thanks particular providers rather than treating species as one moral account. **SW-CG2-20261019-RV-047 - C005, REVISE.** Prior exact **RV043** wording: "Felix's scholarly admiration, care and remembered vulnerability coexist with desired dependence, surveillance, secret lethal means and unequal risk. **SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 — current via RV061.** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through addressed reciprocity, accommodation, selected responsibility, bounded aid and refusal, while attachment also exposes her to leverage, useful-self pressure and concealment guilt. EXTRA09 newly discloses how such ordinary relations take form: a small lie makes room for Cyril, shared responsibility interrupts solitary penance, a gift responds to a particular friend, and receiving kindness can cease to begin with self-reproach. At the new ribbon the narrator explicitly gives joy priority over confusion and inadequacy, followed by an unstumbled thank-you. Initiative includes requesting and attempting shared craft while retaining pleasure beyond utility. These changes are situated, not universal recovery: isolation once provided real relief, present care can pressure choices, concealments and uncomprehended terms remain, and fear, bodily limits and dependence continue.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-004 — current via RV062.** Cyril's procedural duty, earned-worth demands and recipient-specific care coexist with severity, error and selective information. Newly disclosed school scenes show real protective competence before recognition: cash checks prevented a year of embezzlement while a teacher exploited authority and false praise to obstruct detection. His later apology can turn into solitary penance; Monica's claim to present responsibility and Felix's shared work redirect it without curing usefulness-based worth. Loyalty can motivate extraordinary effort yet does not compel every request: voice shame makes him refuse Felix, and a particular homesick listener's need secures one bounded song. Tutoring, dance arrangements and practical accommodation benefit distinct people but cost sleep and let teachers depend on him; passing a minimum does not settle his standards. V09's known Everett remains a familiar junior after painful reverence. After Monica leaves, Cyril chooses delay for the collective rescue: Louis trips him, scraping his palms; Cyril removes the brooch and begins a considered freezing response, choking interrupts it, and induced poisoning overloads the incomplete spell. This remains foreseen dangerous self-risk, not controlled mastery or rescue from an assault on a present Monica. Neither refusal, care, recognition nor research resolves maternal history, rank-bound judgment, magical safety or sustainable self-valuation.

When she cannot solve a threat through combat or mediation, she organizes ordinary rest with others' different skills. Her concern about imposing remains real but does not guarantee nonimposition: expanded cake plans burden Casey, move-day shopping is forceful, and she chooses clothes before making room for Monica's own ribbon choice. Particular adjustments and actual received joy support practiced attention, not a naturally perfect helper or freely informed assent to every plan.

These are actual adopted roles beyond borrowed reassurance. Their exercise remains imperfect: she suppresses an objection to his collection, needs physical/social help, and a genius's thirty-book standard fails to share an ordinary learner's scale. Chosen responsibility therefore changes practice without making adult presentation effortless, all aid informed or every demand safely calibrated.

### Complete current material formulations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 — current via RV071.** Monica's affiliation and agency grow through addressed reciprocity, accommodation, selected responsibility, bounded aid and refusal, while attachment exposes her to leverage, useful-self pressure and concealment guilt. Earlier receiving, asked-for help, craft, chosen filial inquiry, differentiated disclosures and the move toward ordinary life near Lana remain situated gains, not universal recovery. V10 makes received care deliberately available to Rick and Isaac through advice, observation, homecoming, fitting gift and a teaching foundation, while inhibited objection and a genius's thirty-book scale preserve asymmetry. V11 adds an explicit responsibility to understand and, when necessary, stop a pupil's harm. She learns from Isaac's adaptive lesson, refuses ethically compromised exam coaching, acts with an imperfect barrier and elects to recognize a hostile wish she cannot share. MacLagan, Cyril and Bradford supply different adult models; her severe belief that she has done nothing teacher-like does not erase actual earlier care. She tests the new responsibility by asking Will to report excessive demands and promising to speak. His agreement is real; her intimidating pose fails, and Isaac admits recruiting Isabelle to inhibit criticism. A welcomed request for another paper reaches him more directly. Responsibility therefore changes conduct and creates a possible check without guaranteeing effective authority, perfectly calibrated expectations, informed household terms or independence from others' idealization.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; refusal, rescue, addressed agreement and response **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**; shame, wishes and model selection **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION / focalized appraisal**. Preserve **RV065's exact inherited V01–V04 + AFTER04 + specified V05/V06/V08/V09/EXTRA09/V10 set**, with its literal support list and exclusions; **add V11 E003/E012–014/E018/E020**, p-004:P61–198; p-011:P25–41/P239–373; p-012:P63–102; p-014:P198–325; p-015:P177–223/P259–279. No automatic V07 admission. Strongest rival: the teacher role reproduces useful-self performance and replaces one idealization with another. Shame and the failed severe face support that risk; actual ethical refusal, knowingly imperfect rescue, selected models and Will's independent uptake resist total compliance. Opposite completed-equality rival loses admitted leverage and undisclosed travel purpose. Unknown: exercised complaint, costly correction received, sustainable curriculum and household terms. Routes R005/R015/R039/R075/R085/T066/L144/L150–156/Q047/Q058.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-004 — current via RV072.** Cyril's procedural duty, earned-worth demands and recipient-specific care coexist with severity, error and selective information. Earlier checks, thwarted detection, shame-based royal refusal, received beginning of maternal repair, tutoring and corrected dangerous V09 delay remain distinct. That delay occurred after Monica left; he was choked among the remaining helpers, not rescuing a present Monica. V10's privacy-aware coordination and received courage coexist with bodily concealment, leisure-as-duty error and unresolved biological-father concern. V11 demonstrates a changed reception of help: he now accepts a sage pulling the cart where his former self would object, and Highorn's permission enables play against the biological father's remembered prohibition. Rescue draws on familiar address, manual precision and others' strength rather than limitless power. Yet usefulness and rank do not confer every intimacy: Monica's imagined Barney receives a casual trust-face she has not given Cyril, and his narrated anger is directed at his own inadequacy. He continues to entrust her to another, lend the brooch, manage health/cleanup and acknowledge his own junior standing while believing she can grow. His intended birthday wreath is spent before delivery. Real professional maturity, received aid and a still-painful interpersonal distance therefore coexist without proving romance, universal boundaries, safe overload or settled worth.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, with explicit narrator before/after comparison, memory and self-directed anger distinguished from an analyst's diagnosis. Preserve **RV066's exact inherited/corrected V01–V04 + specified AFTER04/V05/V06/V08/V09/EXTRA09/V10 set**; **add V11 E006–007/E009/E011–012/E015/E018**, p-005:P235–273; p-006:P4–262; p-008:P45–162; p-010:P146–217; p-011:P151–182; p-012:P239–274; p-014:P198–273. Explicit acceptance of rank-crossing help, actual play, admitted incompleteness and delegated combat resist it; self-reproach and the imagined friend's privileged intimacy keep insecurity live. Romantic jealousy is a possible further reading, not the narrator's stated self-anger or an established category. V11 makes a downstream learning explicit: losing to Monica teaches him to add connections to money and violence. Neither learning cooperation nor protecting loved people supplies equal consultation, abuse acquittal or the king's undisclosed original commission motive.

### New current-unit claims

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-030 — Raul's friend-directed magic, unequal reception and inherited fear. First registration at V11.** Raul's exuberance is not an exhaustive account of either contentment or harmlessness. Earlier attempted friendship met rejection, while a household hero identity coexists with the public legacy of an evil Thorn Witch. His vegetable/flower work is explicitly meant to make magic through which friends become possible. In present contact he enjoys scolding as the novelty of friendship, accepts the need to stop play for tired companions and supplies real strength and botanical control. Practical failures remain: a separate dosage error magnifies Melissa's sabotage, a spectacular rescue begins before locating Monica, and the mana-absorbing pea remains partly uncontrolled. Family permission and capital defense constrain desired leisure; servants may convert offered rest into further service. At the library, Cyril's entrusted request overcomes Raul's wish to avoid the ancestral book, and Monica's accepted hand counters his particular fear of rejection while holding it. Friendship supplies an actual new reception without guaranteeing universal acceptance, informed touch, safe agriculture or freedom from family control. His ancestor's weapon is genuinely dangerous; inherited fear has a material object as well as a stigma.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-031 — memory-dependent misrecognition as distributed present agency. First registration at V11.** The university fog does not rewind bodies or impose one complete scripted personality. A weak imposed premise—being at Minerva—recruits the target's own memories to change whom they recognize, what repertoire feels available and how they address others. Monica's Barney and Raul's Mary show different relational histories; Cyril's lack of Minerva memory prevents the effect, MacLagan's prior barrier prevents exposure, and Bradford's student-frame rescue remains continuous with his present practice. Louis's student self loses access to acquired adult technique, then his own later invention, returned monocle and Monica's address provide a personally legible contradiction. Names, voice, material objects and other people's actions thus participate in returning a current self. Narration initially withholds identity, then shifts focalizers and supplies explicit corrections, making reader access unequal but not wholly indeterminate. The same formal economy preserves a correct lecture while correcting its supposed domineering motive. Recognition is an enacted relation among memory, cues and helpers, not proof that rank immunizes, regression inevitably makes violence, or the narrator has abandoned factual distinctions.

### Attributable historical error — literal prior wording and support

The complete [original C005/RV073 formulation, support and rivals](#v11-rv073-historical-error) remain above, literally preserved. Its erroneous sentence was: “When a political ally drugs him, he preserves the alliance through blackmail rather than prosecution.” Its erroneous evidence classification was: “drug resistance, blackmail, refusals and admitted leverage **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**”. Both are this producer's reviewed analytical error, not primary-source assertions or current fact. The literal registered support was: “Preserve **RV067's exact inherited V01–V04 + specified V05/V06/V07/V08/V09/EXTRA09/V10 set**, its accident correction and culpability; **add V11 E003–004/E008/E020**, p-004:P114–198/P320–358; p-007:P4–188; p-015:P158–223/P235–279. AFTER04 remains local only.”

The source reason is exact: **SW-LNJP-V11**, chapter four **魔女の秘薬と紳士協定**, original p-007.xhtml (`urn:sw-g2:source/SW-LNJP-V11/primary/item/xhtml/p-007.xhtml`), complete **P52–168** within inspected **P1–188**, especially **P120**: 「僕は毒物に耐性をつけているけれど、流石に魔法薬には抵抗できないからね。今回は飲む前に気づいて良かった」. The rose tradition and silent warning precede cup preservation (P52–79); blackmail follows his permission to remove the cup (P92–110); the confiscated bottle and attributed potency/dose warnings occur in the guest room (P114–121). Stated research interest and refusal follow the technical/legal explanation (P155–168). Lifting the cup preserves evidence; it is not drinking. The scene supplies no physiological victory over an ingested drug.

### C005 — current via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-076

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-005 — current via RV076.** Isaac's real care and intellectual delight coexist with coercive dependency, deception and unequal interpretive power. Replacement coercion, elected memorial, grief, prior culpability and the narrator-confirmed accidental death of Ark remain the causal history; wanted study already existed before the diary. The diary's locally decisive break opens an individual future without restoring his public name or acquitting past means. V10 adds an honored limit, thinking against fear, recipient-specific persuasion, private self-dislike and joyful receipt of homecoming/expectation. V11 specifies a further consequence: making a pie permits recall of his pregnant mother and a promise to an unborn sibling, a childhood he had avoided rather than magically lost. Teaching Glen is genuinely adapted to another learner. When a political ally adulterates his tea to arrange a sexual/dynastic encounter, Isaac detects it before ingestion through the inherited rose warning and Will's silent alert. He prevents removal or spilling from erasing the evidence, identifies the magical preparation, permits the cup's removal and preserves the alliance through blackmail rather than prosecution. The subsequent guest-room scene establishes a confiscated bottle; Will reports its strong aphrodisiac effect and dose risk. Isaac expressly distinguishes his ordinary poison tolerance from inability to resist magical medicine and says he noticed before drinking. He retains the sample for stated research but explicitly rejects using the aphrodisiac on someone; narration relates his desired manner of obtaining a result to smiling before Ark and Monica. This is a particular moral constraint, not a universal consent doctrine: Will still identifies the means/end tendency. Later Isaac sincerely delights in Monica's lecture while admitting he recruited Isabelle to make her criticism harder. His actual apology and received scholarly request do not erase managed access or his undisclosed trip purpose. Chosen regard can constrain one instrument while leaving other coercion and dependency available.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**. Detection, cup preservation, confiscation, blackmail, spoken refusal and admitted leverage are **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**. P120's ordinary-poison tolerance / magical-medicine vulnerability and P160's research purpose are **CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF**: explicit self-reports, not observed physiological capacity or a completed experiment. Will's P117/P121 potency/dose assessment is an attributed technical **CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF**. Prior accident and recovered-memory framing remain **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION**; imagined moral witnesses/private certainty remain **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION / focalized appraisal**. Preserve **RV067's exact inherited V01–V04 + specified V05/V06/V07/V08/V09/EXTRA09/V10 set**, its accident correction and culpability; **retain V11 E003–004/E008/E020**, p-004:P114–198/P320–358; p-007:P4–188; p-015:P158–223/P235–279. Reinspection within p-007's already registered range adds no witness or unit to support. AFTER04 remains local only.

Strongest rival remains dependency transferred from the dead prince to an idealized master, with ethics reduced to winning her approval. Particular moral witnesses support that concern; genuinely remembered family care, already wanted study, preventive detection and an explicitly rejected available method resist a sham-value total. Refusal is spoken and future use is untested; it is not a universal consent doctrine or proof that research custody is harmless. The opposite love-cures-coercion reading still loses actual blackmail, concealed purpose and admitted manipulation. Unknown: a costly limit upheld against desire, later sample research/use, full accountability and mutually informed household/romantic terms. <a id="v12-material-formulations"></a>
### Complete operative material formulations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-003 — current via RV077.** Monica's agency grows through addressed reciprocity, accommodation, chosen responsibilities, bounded aid and refusal, while attachments expose her to leverage, useful-self pressure and concealment guilt. Earlier receiving, differentiated disclosure, filial inquiry, gifts, homecoming and ordinary life near Lana remain situated gains. Giving care does not erase uncalibrated expectations or guarantee equal knowledge. V11's obligation to understand and stop a pupil, ethical refusal, knowingly imperfect rescue and agreed Will complaint route remain; that alliance is still not exercised in V12. This volume adds actual correction of the teacher's own standard: she identifies a formula's failure while leaving Isaac to find its cause, recognizes that the copied Minerva theory-first schedule has deprived this unusually advanced learner of practical knowledge, asks his desired direction and commits to his chosen first original spell. The commitment is real; completion and durable curriculum are not shown.

She also states a release request clearly and creates bodily space when it is refused. Her accepted later offered hand does not authorize the prior grip. Recognition of Isaac's national work and ordinary gratitude do not certify every touch safe. For Norman, recommendation and requested tutoring create access, but the magical penalty on Hubard neither compels teaching nor prevents continuing physical abuse. For Ray, she uses her knowledge of self-blame to prevent harm, tests and revises a failed attack, coordinates finite containment/less destructive sedation and requests specific aftercare. Borrowed villainess practice is effective without graceful mastery or erased fear. Responsibility thus changes both instruction and crisis conduct while remaining fallible about chosen helpers, boundaries and hidden knowledge; it is not completed recovery, perfect protection or a need to become useful enough to deserve affection.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**. Requests, escape, diagnostic test, commitment, letter and performed rescue are **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**; owned pedagogical error and self-blame analogy are **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION / focalized appraisal**. Preserve **RV071's exact inherited support**, its exclusions and all earlier lineage. **SW-CG2-20261019-C-005 — current via RV079.** Isaac's genuine care, intellectual delight and wanted scholarly future coexist with coercive dependence, deception and unequal interpretive power. His replacement coercion, culpability, elected memorial, grief and Ark's narrator-confirmed accidental death remain; the delivered diary opened an individual future without creating his first scholarly pleasure, restoring his name or acquitting old means. V10's honored limit and received homecoming, V11's return of avoided childhood memory and adapted Glen lesson remain. The corrected tea sequence remains exactly preventive: rose warning and Will alert lead him to detect adulteration before drinking, preserve evidence, confiscate the bottle and blackmail a useful ally. Poison tolerance/magical vulnerability and the retained sample's research purpose are attributed statements, not observed drug resistance or completed experiment. His explicit refusal to use the aphrodisiac is a particular moral constraint before imagined Ark/Monica witnesses, compatible with other admitted leverage.

V12 sharply tests that distinction. He recognizes Monica's particular fear yet restrains her without pain and then refuses a clear release request. Her escape and his recognition of error produce real changed distance, apology, promised restraint and a newly accepted hand; later painful cheek-pinching prevents calling the relation generally safe. During the imposed duel he protects through physical ingenuity, personal rage over Ark's face and threatened pain, hiding the expression he does not want her to see. Her request for time flatters his desired pupil-worth until he learns she assumed Will available. Wanting actual magic and accepting an adapted first-spell program are real responses, not proof of completed skill or freedom from dependence. Narration expressly affirms a wish for national welfare beneath covert business calculation. That purpose and his private desire for acceptance from Monica, whose father's death implicates him, coexist without canceling culpability.

Mary's forecast of losing half the visible world elicits thoughts of distancing or watching Monica more closely; neither plan is yet enacted. The final Cyril–Monica scene exposes a response she has not given him. He withdraws instead of confronting them and appeals to the dead Ark for guidance. This is a particular nonintervention and an unsettled feared loss, not a demonstrated general respect for rejection, a confirmed prophecy referent or a renewed elected death. Chosen attachment can constrain one means and motivate a repair while leaving bodily control, concealment and dependent self-worth available.

Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**. Release/refusal/escape, new distance/hand, pain protest, tactics and actual withdrawal are **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**. ### New V12-only claim registrations

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-032 — Ray's regulation under an inherited false explanation. First registration at V12.** Ray's repeated demand to be loved is neither an exhaustive appetite for possession nor a merely harmless verbal tic. His remembered departure, maternal shame and later story of paternal enslavement make affection both urgently wanted and feared as something his own words could coercively produce. Complaints normally vent some negative affect; sincere self-criticism can nevertheless become literal curse overflow. Actual involuntary danger and social stigma reinforce each other. Henrik's killing pressure overwhelms this practiced regulation after party discomfort and exposed shame, while Ray's fear of hurting Frida accelerates the crisis. His interposition for her and flight away from her are protective acts performed in terror, not proof of fearlessness or of selfish carelessness alone.

The late narrator correction gives the reader a different parental history: his mother believed herself unlovable, misread actual love as compulsion and caused real deaths trying to remove it. It does not give that knowledge to Ray, prove all love curses impossible or make his current body safe. Monica's self-blame analogy, finite technical protection, surprise, Raul's enchanted rose and Frida's requested embrace jointly interrupt the crisis. Clear tears are an actual local recovery. Ray's idealization and rapid inference of love remain after rescue; his received beginning is hope and embarrassment, not cured susceptibility, corrected family knowledge or guaranteed love.

Scope **SW-LNJP-V12 only**. Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**, with curse/venting mechanism and final correction **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION**; parental enslavement in Ray's account **CHARACTER_REPORT_BELIEF**; shielding, flight, tears and received contact **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**. Origin **E016/E018/E020–023**, p-013:P153–309; p-014:P4–69/P121–229; p-016:P135–221/P278–331; p-017:P4–41/P75–173/P209–439; p-018:P4–61. Earlier Ray rescues/claims remain antecedents at their own boundaries, not fabricated new origin support. Strongest rival: comedy sentimentalizes a hazardous man and lets trauma excuse demands on women. Real hazards, insults, idealization and cornered recipients support caution; protective self-risk and the narrator's precise regulation/knowledge distinctions resist reducing the person to that demand. Opposite cure-by-love reading loses finite emergency means, no transmitted correction and Frida's explicit refusal to profess love. Unknown: inherited belief revised, enduring regulation, accountable conduct and later chosen attachment. Routes **S-RAY/R107/R109–111/K122/W093–094/T075/L167–170/Q064**.

**SW-CG2-20261019-C-033 — Frida's practical marriage and honest possible attachment. First registration at V12.** Frida's wish for a wealthy spouse arises within household debt, sold possessions and work for siblings/territory. Her financial interest remains real without exhausting her aims or denying a choice of happiness. She judges the feared Albright home by room, meals, wages and treatment, warmly receives anonymous eccentric protection, seeks a shared table and investigates the matchmaking scheme. She resists her brother's protective attempt to impose a return, takes up a duel for herself and Ray, and remains willing to touch him after the curse crisis. These acts preserve her own material and relational purposes rather than making her merely a fearless cure for another person's loneliness.

Her directness is fallible in method and timing: she corners a frightened Ray, and her refusal to pretend love wounds him when others expect a romantic affirmation. The refusal nevertheless distinguishes acquaintance, care, possible liking and love. The hand kiss demonstrates an available form of regard, not an unspoken equivalent of the confession she denies. Praise drawn from his poem answers a particular longing without requiring him to become a jewel. Actual consideration and a conditional future are thus available before romantic certainty, with no narrator guarantee that either marriage or mutual love will follow.

Scope **SW-LNJP-V12 only**. Class **STRONG_INFERENCE**; accounts/actions/words **OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT**, household motives and appraisals **NARRATOR_PRESENTATION / focalized appraisal**, future liking **CHARACTER_EXPECTATION**. Origin **E016/E018/E020/E022–023**, p-013:P4–88/P250–309; p-014:P121–199; p-016:P73–134/P198–220; p-017:P4–41/P75–173/P209–274/P388–405; p-018:P4–61. Strongest rival: practical marriage is only financial opportunism reframed as care, or Frida is a device whose composure exists to repair Ray. Prior sacrifice, independently voiced loneliness, resistance to her own brother, fact-finding and refusal of a false love claim preserve aims that exceed his need. Opposite flawless-honesty reading loses fear-inducing pursuit and the pain her timing causes. Unknown: changed affection, compatibility, economic implementation and negotiated boundaries. Routes **S-FRIDA/R107–108/R112/K124/T074/T076/L167/L170/Q064**.
