### C006 — operative candidate via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-081

Louis combines professional fit, self-interest, practical care and coercion. His correction of a heartlessness model in V04 remains real, and recognizing attachment can still justify exclusion. The stopped V07 memory-sealing test remains unperformed and barred. V09's disagreement is not a secret lesson: Monica diverts his carriage and attacks remotely before leaving, while his prepared magical/physical advantages act against remaining helpers, including tripping and choking Cyril. A coalition delays rather than overpowers him; admitted defeat and later punishment retain unequal costs. His acknowledgment of Glen's future potential and need for development is actual professional judgment, not completed foundations or safe training. V11 explicitly connects defeat to adding connections to money and violence: he trusts Owen after earlier coerced betrayal, privately funds support, uses Lin's limited communication and relies on unlike partners to protect family/capital. Temporary fog withdraws access to acquired adult technique; recognizing his later invention helps return it. Dropping Monica to a sealing task, proposing punitive smoke and needing other adults' criticism remain counterevidence to redemption. Publication advice, sewing, family concern and Rosalie's enforced rest are real context/recipient differences, not general consultation or acquittal.

ANOTHER-UPPER discloses an earlier, uneven formation. Survival labor and anti-servitude violence precede a conceived career; annotated instruction, diagnostic practice, defeat and Major's correction develop skill rather than reveal effortless mastery. Wanted marmalade, specific admiration, independently offered help, received gifts and recognized teaching open valued relations before sage ambition. Respecting Owen's exam time, acknowledging Major's contribution and promising to accept Rosalie's refusal are real early reciprocal practices. They coexist with intimidation, chosen excess and distorted reception. Supporting Rosalie's independent medical departure, Row's account of respect for a beloved's other bonds, and Lionel's requested instruction give his acquired public style and later vocational plan concrete origins. Romance changes selected conduct and purpose without making injury, coercive pedagogy or retaliatory cruelty necessary or safe.

The newly disclosed early cooperation **qualifies a linear development story**; it does not contradict V11's narrator-attributed later lesson or prove that he had already mastered its adult application. Learner, lover, friend, pupil and commander are connected but unequal contexts. Neither the early refusal promise nor protection of family establishes equal briefing, safe teaching, accountability to all affected people or the king's undisclosed original commission motive.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE, grounded in OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT and NARRATOR_PRESENTATION, with speakers' appraisals and actual gifts/requests distinguished. **Support/scope:** preserve **RV074's exact inherited RV060 set: V01–V04 + specified V06/V07/V08/V09, including all corrected actors, sequence and V09 E022; plus specified V11 E001/E010/E014/E016–018/E021** as registered in RV074. ### SW-CG2-20261019-C-034 — Rosalie: constraint, independent vocation and received affection

**First registration: ANOTHER-UPPER.** Rosalie's academic excellence is a real capacity beside a persistent physiological limit that effort has not removed. Her father's status, early contempt and withdrawn instruction help turn that limit into pressure to excel everywhere else, while his unspoken remorse does not reach her as repair. She can judge Louis's local conduct independently, act to protect him, envy his freedom, accept a particular apology and desire him without approving his methods. Choosing medicine does not negate Minerva or merely implement Louis's plan: she explicitly retains its value, thanks him and leaves. Their reciprocal confession preserves her chosen separate training and an offered refusal rather than requiring her continued availability as his restraint. Guilt and misreading persist; neither romance nor changing fields completes a cure or familial reconciliation.

The enduring guilt and her uncommunicated preference contradict closure. **Uncertainty/triggers:** actual medical qualification is known later at its earlier admitted adult boundary, but this childhood scene does not itself complete it; no father/daughter reconciliation or received correction of the prince ideal is shown here. <a id="another-lower-c022"></a>
### C022 — operative candidate via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-083

Glen's wish to become useful and heroic does not make his cheerful presentation transparent evidence of ease. V06 dramatizes how class contempt and praise-seeking exposed an eleven-year-old with very large reserves but weak foundations to Hubard's abusive “safe” practice: greater reserves prolonged pain rather than supplying mastery. The then-current Glen concealed pain to spare others, received and practiced specific foundational help, adapted care to recipients and cooperated under real danger. Neither the earlier runaway nor later learning proves innate violence, a painless cure or a single cause for every cheerful act.

ANOTHER-LOWER supplies the intervening confinement and release: a child who fears repeating uncontrolled injury chooses apprenticeship within Louis's restricted alternatives. The corps' actual containment and Owen's credible rescue promise let him experience failure without being feared as a dangerous asset, while renewed competitiveness does not remove weak theory or painful discipline. Gratitude toward Louis coexists with resentment and a wish to protect his reputation. Glen's medical/cooking reciprocity with Rosalie is independently real; his attempt to protect her from remembered pain acts without assent, on a false suicide inference, and his incomplete formula suppresses more than intended. Compassion and insufficient foundations therefore can produce coercive harm as well as useful help. His confession/apology and Rosalie's refusal to prosecute are actual dispositions, followed by supervised magic, not proof that the intervention was harmless or all future boundaries learned.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE from directly narrated retrospective/present scenes and observed acts; prophecy remains reported counterfactual, not fulfilled national destruction. **Exact inherited support:** **SW-LNJP-V06 E011–013/E015/E019–021/E026/E029**, principally p-007:P4–143 and p-008:P4–116, with the V06 repair's reserves/panic sequence retained. **Add SW-LNJP-ANOTHER-LOWER E004–006/E016/E019–020/E022/E027–028**, especially p-005:P52–300; p-010:P82–134; p-011:P221–289; p-012:P4–220; p-013:P319–326; p-014:P240–269; p-015:P67–72. V05's repaired antecedent, AFTER04's interrupted account and V07–V12 later local outcomes retain their own evidence classes and are not silently retro-registered.

Chosen cooking, medical repayment, desire for both loved people to be happy, moral hesitation before sealing and actual gratitude resist that account. **Counterpressure:** benevolent self-appraisal can still overrule another person's mind/body; good intent and recovery do not answer permission. **Uncertainty/triggers:** what later instruction changes his formula judgment and recipient listening; whether supervision is internalized; exact wider prophecy conditions; sustained freedom to disagree with Louis. <a id="another-lower-c034"></a>
### C034 — operative candidate via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-084

Rosalie's academic excellence is a real capacity beside a persistent physiological limit that effort has not removed. Her father's status, early contempt and withdrawn instruction turn that limit into pressure to excel elsewhere; his privately recognized wrongdoing does not itself reach her as repair. She can judge Louis's conduct, act to protect him, envy freedom, receive particular care and desire him without approving every method. Choosing medicine retains her education's value and her independent aim rather than merely implementing his plan. Reciprocal affection and separate training in Upper do not cure guilt or complete familial reconciliation.

Lower enacts medical qualification, chosen corps work and professional boundaries. Her skilled priorities and acquired knowledge persist under a magical loss of personal recall; that continuity is not proof of a timeless uninjured self or clinical cure. Both lovers' letters were blocked by a forgotten paternal order. Acquired gentility, gossip, an overheard sentence and Louis's protective withholding make her mistaken status explanation causally intelligible. She tries to release a man she believes burdened, later receives the actual marriage condition and his chosen-burden explanation, names her preference for the familiar unruly boy and claims her own years of waiting. The carriage supplies received lover-to-lover repair that appointment and spell release alone did not.

Birdland's aging reserves finally give him experiential access to her limit; his regret, rescinded letter order and wedding attendance do not amount to a directly narrated father/daughter acknowledgment. Her legal choices toward Glen and Owen, her invitation to Rutherford and her chosen village visit preserve agency beyond being Louis's restraint or reward. Burden guilt and initial protested handling remain alongside explicit romantic delight and reciprocal contact. Consent/pleasure in particular acts cannot be generalized to all touch or all protective decisions.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE grounded in OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT/NARRATOR_PRESENTATION, with her self-appraisals, father's private reasoning, received speech and magical mechanism distinguished. **Exact inherited support:** **ANOTHER-UPPER E006/E010–011/E013/E015–020**, original locators at [first registration](#another-upper-new-claims), including no-regret medical departure and early affirmative confession. **Add SW-LNJP-ANOTHER-LOWER E008–011/E013/E015–016/E019–020/E022–025/E027–029**; decisive p-006:P82–244, p-008:P224–301, p-009:P128–261, p-013:P4–25/P225–294/P298–433, p-014:P119–153/P240–322, p-015:P21–44/P119–162. Later adult medical practice remains at its originally admitted witness, not invented earlier support.

**Strongest rival:** vocation and love redirect a gifted woman's work into servicing a powerful man. Continued care and self-blame sustain concern; explicitly chosen medicine, professional address, her refusals, voiced preference, shared waiting and independent social/legal choices resist reduction. **Opposite rival:** real love and recovered memory solve the paternal injury and all burdens. Undelivered paternal remorse, recurrent guilt and the lovers' need for actual talk contradict it. **Uncertainty/triggers:** directly received father/daughter acknowledgment, future terms on protective bodily/mental intervention, career choices under new conflicting demands. **Routes:** R010/R119/R123 retained; R129–132/R134/K132–135/T083/T085/L192–195/L198–201/Q067. No new prediction of already known marriage or adult competence.

<a id="another-lower-c035"></a>
### C035 — operative candidate via SW-CG2-20261019-RV-085

Major's legitimate concern with Louis's violence coexists with a teacher's willingness to answer a disliked pupil's serious questions. Meticulous correction and continuing study let her supply newer connections than a revered master's formula; her initial sense that his achievement is irreplaceable limits the possibilities she sees. Louis's evidence-based respect and public supervisor credit let her receive recognition of actual work without an official epithet. She recommends broader training elsewhere and retires for her own reasons. Neither contribution nor recognition exempts him from her standards.

Lower demonstrates continuing expertise through a current low-leakage barrier protecting children and artisans amid dangerous mana. It cannot prevent the failing floor's impact; Louis's structural support, evacuation and corps assistance complement rather than expose her as useless. He identifies himself to her pupils as that excellent teacher's student, and she credits conduct worthy of the transmitted name. Their reciprocal recognition enacts the earlier prospect of carrying teaching forward. Her new pupils' questions and her continuing study show an independent educational life, not a career completed by his gratitude.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE, observed operation/credit distinguished from teacher appraisal and IMAGE_REFERENCE afterfacts. **Exact inherited support:** **ANOTHER-UPPER E007/E009/E012–013/E016/E022**, original locators and founder/retirement distinctions at [first registration](#another-upper-new-claims). **Add SW-LNJP-ANOTHER-LOWER E021/E028/E031**, p-012:P278–354, p-015:P30–44 and reference p306. **Strongest rival:** sentimental praise compensates for an unaccomplished career and decorates Louis's success. Structural limits, actual complementarity and other teachers' abuse contradict it. **Uncertainty/triggers:** future credit, particular novice uptake and revision of techniques remain open; no official Barrier Witch award or claim that the student invented every component. **Routes:** R121/R135/K137/W106/T084/L196/C036.

Their observed timing, temporary supports and casualty prevention resist that reading. **Uncertainty/exclusion:** the argument concerns these operations, not a universal doctrine of all magic or moral permission for Louis's retaliation/teaching. <a id="post16-c037"></a>
### SW-CG2-20261019-C-037 — another person's answer changes the achievement

**Initial/current formulation:** Across these diagnostic cases, forming a benevolent or devoted purpose, making it available to a particular recipient, obtaining an intelligible answer and respecting that answer are distinct achievements. Real care can occur without shared comprehension, while effective protection or a sincere wish can still appropriate another person's future. Development sometimes consists in receiving or asking more successfully, sometimes in revising an already competent practice for this recipient, and sometimes only in the reader learning a capacity that existed earlier. No uniform healing sequence, total-disclosure ethic or inevitable reciprocal romance follows.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE, with OBSERVABLE_TEXT_EVENT, NARRATOR_PRESENTATION, attributed belief and retrospective comparison distinguished. Normative evaluation is limited to actually represented permission, refusal, imposed bodily/mental cost, recipient knowledge and available response; it is not an invented storyworld statute or a universal authorial doctrine. **Scope:** the admitted sixteen-work retrospective boundary, latest continuing events V12. Registered diagnostic support is specifically **V01:E008–010/E017; V02:E003/E017/E028–029; V03:E016/E037–040; V04:E007/E024–025/E037; AFTER04:E017/E024–025; V05:E027; V06:E026; V07:E008–009/E013–014; V08:E012–015/E031–032; V09:E001–003/E018–020; EXTRA09:E012–014/E025–026; V10:E019/E024; V11:E008/E020; V12:E005/E019/E023–024; ANOTHER-UPPER:E019; ANOTHER-LOWER:E010/E024/E028–029**. This list is new C037 support, never an extension by implication of C003/C004/C005/C012/C023/C025/C028/C034.

The decisive contrast is received change. Myra's V04 intent becomes V05 welcome; Sheila's hidden words can be confirmed without Byron having read them; Ark's surviving diary must be delivered and read beyond Isaac's habitual accusation. Conversely, V12's narrated correction cannot cure Ray because he is not told, and Isaac's inward remorse cannot be heard by Monica as an apology. Louis/Rosalie's actual carriage explanation repairs something that spell release and appointment did not; Birdland's private regret remains differently situated. Lana's ribbon scene gives explicitly changed reception without a universal cure. Lin's Kara-specific accommodation and an embraced body without warmth show that neither human sameness nor complete understanding is necessary for actual attachment.

**Strongest explanatory rival:** these are conventional delayed-recognition and courtship devices whose pleasing outcomes are the literary end, so recipient distinctions overstate an ethical pattern. It explains surprise and pleasure, but not why the text repeatedly withholds the answer an apparent completion invites: an unreceived letter, a noncontractual spirit guest, a refused release, conditional possible liking and an explicitly private correction. **Opposite rival:** every unshared meaning makes care sham or every devotion merely control. Actual received gratitude, practical benefits, offered refusal, chosen study, the once-only song and particular promises defeat that totalization. Real pleasure can survive an incomplete shared account.

**Resistant evidence and uncertainty:** danger can require intervention before assent is obtainable; the brooch preservation and Ray emergency do not become identical to using a sleeping child as a contract stone. Some humor grants favorites more bodily latitude than the proposed ideal of answerable care would predict. The claim explains a recurrent tension, not universal compliance with a rule. Future recipient disagreement, a sustained correction costing a desired result, a clarified romantic category or a different stated account of development could narrow particular applications. Do not infer a clinical profile, complete agency or final relationship from this cross-comparison.

**Affected routes:** the six character/ensemble files, two central dyads and narrative specialist at [the product map](SW_CG2_MASTER_LEDGER.md#post16-products); master T088 and L203; integrated synthesis. No prior wording exists and no initial RV event is fabricated.

<a id="post16-c038"></a>
### SW-CG2-20261019-C-038 — an available option does not settle authority over its cost

**Initial/current formulation:** Technical skill and institutional arrangements materially change what actions are available; their success does not alone settle whose purpose should govern, whose permission is required or where the remaining costs fall. The diagnostic operations distribute agency through different capacities, resources, timing and information. They can enable a less destructive choice while preserving coercion, unequal credit or unfinished accountability. Neither self-sufficient genius, virtue measured by reserves, institutions as wholly benevolent, nor institutions as merely fraudulent explains the comparison.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE from narrated mechanics, observed operations, stated institutional conditions and separately attributed judgments; bounded NORMATIVE_EVALUATION uses represented harm, choice, authorization and displaced burdens. **Scope/support:** sixteen-work review boundary with the following registered diagnostic cases, not automatic support from every scene: **V01:E021; V02:E006/E027–029; V03:E037–038; V04:E034/E036; V05:E025–026; V06:E012/E025/E027; V07:E006/E011–014; V08:E028–029; V09:E003–006/E012–016; EXTRA09:E013/E024; V10:E001/E016–019; V11:E005–007/E017–018; V12:E010–013; ANOTHER-UPPER:E009–010/E013/E016; ANOTHER-LOWER:E006/E020–021**. Original local statements of rules remain their own speakers and histories; a proposed reform or factory is not completed implementation.

The strongest mechanisms are not interchangeable. Glen's reserves prolong painful practice; Owen and the corps later supply credible containment. Major's low-leakage shelter works while the floor still needs Louis's structure. The Grail's controls are genuine while Albert's sample supplies false parentage provenance. Correct calculation does not authenticate sabotaged ingredients. A sealed magician can continue physical abuse. V10's restraint-enabled penal alternative gives technical success real institutional value, without proving a completed just system. These differences connect capability to available agency without dissolving either into sentiment.

**Strongest rival:** constraints merely make adventure solutions exciting, and the critic turns technical exposition into an unearned political program. The reading retains the excitement and makes no claim of a comprehensive authorial program. Its narrower inference is supported because the narratives themselves distinguish a result from a residual wrong, withheld permission or displaced cost. **Opposite rival:** all expertise and institutional care are domination. Actual rescue, chosen work, professional pride, material leverage removed, acknowledged limits and revised teaching contradict that account. The best-supported claim is conditional, not a verdict against precision, payment, office or power.

**Resistant evidence and uncertainty:** exceptional force sometimes is decisive; collaborative dependence is not proof that every contribution has equal weight. A threatened child has fewer alternatives than a later adult, while coercion does not erase every culpable act. The complete legal, economic and scientific systems are not supplied. Safe harvests, Norman's actual schooling, reform implementation, spirit consent/custody and the original royal commission remain open. Reconsider at an independently authenticated sample, a tested safe alternative, a recipient's contrary account, completed reform or a demonstrably solitary operation defeating the specified explanation.

**Affected routes:** expertise/institutions specialist, Power/Dependence and Louis–Rosalie studies, master T089/L204 and synthesis. Old C001/C006/C009/C020–022/C024/C026/C029/C034–036 retain their exact registered scopes. No prior wording exists and no RV086 is warranted merely for this new comparison.

<a id="post16-c039"></a>
### SW-CG2-20261019-C-039 — narrative access is unequal; its ethical effect is not uniform

**Initial/current formulation:** Changing focalization, delayed revelation, Japanese speech and silence, named roles, typography and visual juxtaposition distribute access to intentions and consequences. This can let a reader recognize an intended act before its addressee, distinguish a settled narrated event from a participant's false account, or perceive a gap without receiving its answer. Comic closeness can accommodate frightening people and sustain ordinary pleasures while unevenly reducing attention to costs. These formal effects warrant scene-specific ethical tension, not uniformly unreliable narration, a single authentic private self or a total authorial doctrine inferred from tone.

**Class:** STRONG_INFERENCE / FORMAL_PROPOSITION; direct wording, ruby and composition are evidence, while their function is interpretive inference. **Scope/support:** the admitted sixteen-work retrospective boundary; diagnostic registered support **V01:E008–011/E021/E023; V02:E025–030; V03:E016–020/E041–042; V04:E020/E037; AFTER04:E017/E021/E024; V05:E020–021/E031; V06:E023/E025–029; V07:E009/E012–014; V08:E009–015/E022/E032; V09:E012/E018; EXTRA09:E011–012/E017/E025; V10:E003/E017/E024; V11:E011–014/E019; V12:E015/E019/E021–024 and native p308–309; ANOTHER-UPPER:E013/E019–021; ANOTHER-LOWER:E010–011/E022–025**. Images do not establish unspoken thought absent textual support; afterwords remain production testimony or explicit hypothetical play, not enacted story.

Diagnostic distinctions constrain the inference: V04 explicitly times Eliane's residual curse; V09 explicitly denies suicidal intent; V12 gives Ray's parental correction to readers alone, but names Cyril's self-deception without naming its final affect. Kayan's base 「断罪して」/ruby 「救つて」 preserves opposed wishes in one appeal, without making either happen. V12's corridor pair gives bodies and distance to three unequal appraisals rather than a romantic verdict. Thornley's later pig fear and Lower's excessive retaliation/cover resist harmless-comedy inference without proving that the work intends one hidden condemnation.

**Strongest rival:** these are efficient comic, mystery and courtship conventions; interpretation should credit their pleasure without turning every device into an ethical claim. This reading does credit it. The narrower proposition concerns repeated causal dependence on who can hear or know what, and the actual mismatch between tones of affection and represented costs. **Opposite rival:** every surface is false, so any private or critical interpretation is equally authorized. Explicit narrator corrections, material consequences and the distinctions among report, memory, inference and direct event refute that skepticism. Private viewpoints themselves can be wrong.

**Resistant evidence and uncertainty:** a joke or mathematically beautiful operation can be valuable without carrying the recurrent argument; supporting aims resist a universal key. Some narrative framings grant latitude the comparative ethical argument cannot wholly reconcile. The claim retains that tension rather than attributing a complete writer psychology. Future explicit correction, changed tone with a recipient's account, clarified prophecy or a different visual/textual relation could narrow an application. Unidentified figures and private knowledge remain unjoined.

**Affected routes:** narrative/voice specialist, both dyads, character/ensemble closeouts, master T090/L205 and synthesis. C008 remains its V01-only origin and C031 its V11-only fog argument; C039 is a genuinely new retrospective formal claim, not their quiet expansion. No prior wording or initial RV event exists.
